## Phone & Projection

Phone
The Bluetooth capable Infotainment system and steering wheel controls allows hands-free calling and receiving of phone calls﻿.
A compatible Bluetooth phone must be connected to use the phone app.
CAUTION
In order to minimize driver distraction, prior to driving and while the vehicle is parked:
- Complete all pairing of mobile devices.
- Familiarize yourself with all system functionality and controls.
- Organize contact and call lists clearly and avoid duplicates.
NOTE
Not all Bluetooth phones support all functions.
To Launch the Phone App:
Tap  App Library    on the Infotainment screen.
Tap  Phone App   .
NOTE
When there is an ongoing call, the user can also click the phone sticky widget on the Homescreen to open the Phone App.
Using the Phone App:
The following tabs/pages are available on the phone app:
Recents – Shows all call history when phone connected
Contacts – Lists all available contacts from phone
Keypad – Allows manual entry and dialing of phone numbers
Settings – Provides options to enable/disable Do Not Disturb and manage auto-reply messages
NOTE
Upon initial connection, please allow time for all contacts to be synced from phone.
Steering Wheel phone button behavior:
Phone app  functions can be controlled via buttons on the right wheel of the steering wheel, see >   Steering Wheel > Steering Wheel Switches  .
Recent
The Recent tab lets the user see past calls from their phone. The recent call list can be viewed by tapping on the Recent tab at the Phone app window. Tapping on any entry in the Recent list will initiate a call to that number.
Types of Call Icons:
: Incoming Call
: Declined or Missed Call
: Outgoing Call
Contacts
Phone contacts can be viewed by tapping on the  Contacts  tab on the  Phone app  window. Contacts stored in the memory of a paired phone will be synced to the vehicle’s contact list. The contact list is synced each time the phone is connected to the system.
NOTE
The search box appears in its disabled state if there is data indexing process.
To search for a Contact:
Tap  Search    icon on the contacts page.
Enter Contact Name/Number in the  Search  field.
NOTE
Previously searched contacts will be displayed in recent searches.
Up to 10 recent searches are displayed in the recent list below the search text field.
Keypad
Dial the phone number manually using the keypad.
To call a number, enter the phone number using the keypad. If the number entered matches with a saved contact, it will show the contact details on the screen.
NOTE
Pressing and holding “0” will enter “+” in the text box.
Incoming Call
During an incoming call, the caller ID will appear on the Infotainment screen. If the contact is saved in the user’s phone then the person’s name and number is also displayed. To answer an incoming call, tap on the green Accept button on the screen or short press the right scroll wheel on the steering wheel when the green Accept button on the screen is highlighted. To Decline a call, tap on the red Decline button on the screen or short press the right scroll wheel on the steering wheel when the red Decline button on the screen is highlighted.
The following options are available during a call:
Full Incoming Call Window
Contact information
Accept Call
Reject Call
Incoming Call Banner
When a new incoming call is present, an Incoming Call notification will appear on the Infotainment screen. The notification will contain a caller ID, Accept and Decline options. Taping on the notification will take the user to the incoming call window.
NOTE
If the Do Not Disturb function is enabled, incoming calls will be automatically rejected and responded to with the selected message.
Active call / Single Call:
While a call is active, the call duration and contact details will be shown.
Expand Keypad
End Call
Mute Call
Switch to Handset Device
NOTE
When the user exits the Active call window to use other apps or go to the Home page on screen, the active call window minimizes and docks a card on top of the Infotainment screen.
Incoming Call over an Active Call:
Accepting a second call will put the active call on Hold and switch to the new call. The call on hold will be displayed on the right side of the Active Call window.
Tap on the Caller ID to switch back to the call on Hold. This will put the second call on hold and it will be displayed on the right side of the Active Call window.
Settings
Phone settings can be viewed by tapping on the  Settings  tab on the  Phone app  window. This allows the user to adjust the following settings:
Tap to toggle  Do Not Disturb  to OFF/ON
Select a present Auto Replay Message
Create custom messages
NOTE
Up to 20 custom messages can be saved, each limited to 160 characters.
USB Ports & Power Outlets
USB Ports
The vehicle is equipped with 4 USB ports:
Front row: Two USB ports are located on the front of the center console, below the center air vents.
Rear row: Two USB ports are located on the rear of the center console, below the rear air vents.
Front USB Ports
1 Type A USB Port  . Maximum output power: 7.5W.
1 Type C USB Port  . Maximum output power: 60W.
NOTE
Type A USB Port   can be used to transfer data (i.e. play music, video) and charge devices.
Type C USB Port   can be used only for charging.
Rear USB Ports
1 Type C USB Port  . Maximum output power: 60W.
1 Type C USB Port  . Maximum output power: 18W.
WARNING
It is recommended to connect an input device only when the vehicle is stationary. Trying to connect an input device while driving may cause distraction and lead to an accident.
CAUTION
Connecting multiple devices using a USB hub is not recommended, this can prevent connected devices from charging or from being recognized by the infotainment system.
Unplug all accessories when not in use to prevent an electrical short if the unconnected end contacts another power source or liquid.
NOTE
All rear USB ports are for charging devices only.
Always disconnect the USB cable after use.
Power Outlet
The vehicle is equipped with 2 power outlets.
Front Power Outlet   is located inside the center armrest compartment.
Rear Power Outlet   is located in the left side of the rear luggage compartment.
To access the low voltage outlet, remove the protective cap. The power outlet is suitable for accessories with a voltage of 12V to 24V (120W to 250W).
WARNING
Close the Power Outlet cap when not in use.
Do not allow children to use or play with the Power Outlet. Children may get seriously injured.
Do not use the Power Outlet for electric heaters while sleeping. Doing so can cause serious injury or death.
Do not insert foreign objects into the Power Outlet. Doing so can cause serious injury or death.
Do not use faulty electrical appliances. Doing so can cause serious injury or death.
Do not insert inappropriate or badly fitting plugs into the Power Outlet. Doing so can cause serious injury or death.
Do not jump start a vehicle using the Power Outlet.
The power socket and an accessory’s connector can become hot. Touching hot surfaces may cause burning.
CAUTION
Some electronic devices and accessories can cause electronic interference when plugged into a Vehicle’s Power Outlet. These devices may cause excessive audio static and cause a malfunction in other electronic systems or devices used in the vehicle.
Use devices equipped with batteries with reverse current protection. Otherwise the current from the battery may flow into the vehicle’s electrical system and may cause system malfunction.
Leaving electrical equipment plugged in for an extended period of time while the vehicle is off will drain the battery. Always unplug electrical equipment when not in use.
Wireless Charger (if equipped)
The wireless charger provided in the front console of the vehicle can charge Qi 2.0 compatible devices wirelessly. To charge a compatible device, place the device on the charging pads to start charging the device. The wireless charger can be set to On or Off depending on the user’s preference.
The charger will show charging status and any relevant notifications of the phone on the Infotainment screen. It also reminds the user if the phone is left behind in the car upon exit (phone forget reminder).
NOTE
The vehicle must be on and all doors closed to enable wireless charging.
CAUTION
Do not place objects between the phone and the charger (for example, credit cards, key cards, coins, keys, metal objects, etc.).
Foreign Object Detection (FOD) is designed to detect objects before power transfer. Foreign objects placed on the charging pad may interrupt charging or cause a charging fault.
Near Field Communication (NFC) enabled cards may get damaged if kept on the wireless charger pad.
Avoid spilling water or other liquids on the wireless charger as excessive or repeated spillage can cause malfunction. (The charger is IP54 rated).
NOTE
To correctly charge the phone it must be in direct contact with the wireless charger.
The Lightning Bolt   icon in the status bar indicates when wireless charging is active.
NOTE
When the device is fully charged, the Lightning bolt icon disappears and a notification is displayed.
How to Turn On/Off Wireless Charging:
Tap  App Library    on the touchscreen.
Or, Slide down from top of touchscreen to open Notification Center.
Tap  Settings    >  Vehicle Settings    >  Wireless Charging .
Toggle to turn  On / Off  wireless charging.
To turn On/Off Wireless Charging from quick controls of the touchscreen:
Slide down from top of touchscreen to open Notification Center.
Tap  Wireless Charging    button   to turn  On / Off  wireless charging.
Wireless Charging Fault
If there is a fault with the wireless charging system, a corresponding warning will be shown on the touchscreen. Contact a VinFast Service Center.
Android Auto
Android Auto is used to mirror features of an Android device, such as a smartphone, on the vehicle’s Infotainment screen. Android Auto can be launched on the Infotainment screen when a compatible device is connected.
Connecting to Android Auto
Android Auto only can be launched by connecting a compatible device either via a USB cable or Bluetooth.
For more information about Bluetooth connections, see >   Settings > Devices Settings  .
Note
Setup of Android Auto can be done only when the vehicle is in Park (P).
To launch Android Auto via USB Cable:
Connect the android device to the vehicle via a USB cable, plug a USB into your vehicle’s USB port, then plug the other end of the cable into your phone.
Tap  App Libiary    >  Android Auto    on the infotainment screen.
Follow the infotainment screen instructions to connect  Android Auto.
Once connected the Android Auto icon will show active on the status bar.
To Disconnect Android Auto via USB Cable:
Disconnect the USB cable from the phone or vehicle.
Apple CarPlay
Apple CarPlay is used to mirror features of an iOS device, such as a smartphone, on the vehicle’s Infotainment screen. Apple CarPlay can be launched on the Infotainment screen when a compatible device is connected.
Connecting to Apple CarPlay
Apple CarPlay only can be launched by connecting a compatible device either via a USB cable or Bluetooth.
For more information about Bluetooth connections, see >   Settings > Devices Settings  .
Note
Setup of Apple CarPlay can be done only when the vehicle is in Park (P).
To launch Apple CarPlay via USB Cable:
Connect the iOS device to the vehicle via a USB cable, plug a USB into your vehicle’s USB port, then plug the other end of the cable into your phone.
Tap  App Libiary    >  Apple CarPlay    on the infotainment screen.
Follow the infotainment screen instructions to connect Apple CarPlay.
Once connected, the Apple CarPlay icon will show active on the status bar.
To Disconnect Apple CarPlay via USB Cable:
Disconnect the USB cable from the phone or vehicle.

## VinFast App

Overview and Key Features
The VinFast App is a mobile application that provides various useful services for VinFast vehicles.
The information about the VinFast App’s features in this user guide is for reference only. The actual interface and functions may vary depending on the region, vehicle model, and release time.
Some of the highlights of the VinFast App may include:
Viewing battery charging status and vehicle condition.
Using remote control features such as the air conditioning system, door locks, etc.
Searching for charging station information, scheduling service appointments, or requesting roadside assistance.
Planning trips and sending them directly to the vehicle (if available).
Viewing the current vehicle status.
Accessing the user manual.
CAUTION
The VinFast App requires either a WiFi or cellular connection. Data fees may apply.
Registration and Sign In
To use the VinFast App, you need to download the app and register an account.
To download the App:
Visit the Apple App Store (iOS) or Google Play Store (Android).
Search for and download the VinFast App.
To register:
Open the app and tap Sign Up on the login page.
Read and agree to the Terms & Conditions.
Enter a Username (email address) and create a password.
Check your inbox for a verification email and confirm your registration.
Set up Fingerprint / Face ID (optional).
To Sign In:
Tap the VinFast icon on your phone to open the app.
Enter your registered email and password.
Forgotten Password:
If you forget your password, you can reset it by:
Tapping Forgot Password at the bottom of the login page.
Entering your registered email address.
Tapping Reset Password.
Checking your email and following the instructions to create a new password.
Vehicle and User Management
Guest Homepage
Before owning a VinFast vehicle, you can use the app to explore detailed specifications and services. When you launch the app and register, the guest home page will appear.
Linking VinFast App with a vehicle
Vehicle Owner
When purchasing a VinFast vehicle, the dealer will assist you in creating a VinFast ID, which identifies you as the vehicle owner.
Use the email address registered with your VinFast ID when creating your account in the VinFast App for automatic linking to your vehicle.
If the vehicle information does not sync automatically in the app or if your vehicle is not linked to your account, please contact your VinFast dealer for support.
For Secondary Drivers
You can also add or remove secondary drivers to use the VinFast App.
Contact the vehicle owner and ask to be added as a driver.
The owner will use Role Management to add the additional driver’s account to their vehicle.
The additional driver should provide the same email address they used to register the VinFast App.
The vehicle owner will send an invitation for the additional driver to add a vehicle.
The additional driver may:
Access and control owner’s vehicle using the VinFast App.
Add / Remove one additional driver account.
Home - VinFast ID. Tap on General Info on the Guest Homepage for more information.

## Climate Control System

Climate Controls
The climate control system cools, heats, filters and dehumidifies the air in the passenger compartment. Generally, the system works most efficiently when the windows are closed.
Climate Control Overview
Below is an overall description of air conditioning control interface on the vehicle.
To open Climate Control Menu:
Tap  Fan Speed    button on the Climate bar, which is part of the bottom bar.
Front Zone Controls:
Front Zone Controls provide some function follow:
Air distribution control
Auto mode
Air conditioner (AC)
Maximum air conditioning
Recirculation mode
Fan speed adjustment
Temperature controls
Temperature synchronization (Sync)
Air ionization
Seat controls (Control cooling level) (if available)
Rear Zone Controls:
Rear Zone Controls provide some function follow:
Fan speed adjustment
Cooling/Air-Conditioning
To turn on/off the Air Conditioning system:
Tap on the  A/C  icon to turn on the air conditioning, tap again to turn it off.
Maximum Cooling
To turn on/off the Maximum Cooling:
Tap on  MAX AC  icon to turn on maximum cooling mode, tap again to turn it off.
NOTE
Water may drip from underneath the vehicle when it is in cooling mode. Traces of water on the ground are normal and are not a sign of leakage or malfunction.
If no cooling is required, switch off the A/C cooling system to increase range efficiency.
Air Vents
Front Windshield Defrosting/Defogging Vents
Front Side Windows Defrosting/Defogging Vents
Driver Side Air Vent
Front Central Air Vent
Passenger Side Air Vent
Front Footwell Air Vent
Rear Central Air Vent
Note
The air distribution is adjusted by pressing the appropriate buttons on the Infotainment screen.
Adjustable Vents
To adjust the driver and passenger side air vents
Push the air outlet adjustment knob up, down, left and right to adjust the direction of the air outlet.
Push the air outlet adjustment knob outward to the maximum position to close the air outlet.
To adjust the front central air vent
Push the air outlet adjustment knob up, down, left and right to adjust the direction of the air outlet.
Push the air outlet adjustment knob outward to the maximum position to close the air outlet.
To adjust the rear central air vent
Push the air outlet adjustment knob up, down, left and right to adjust the direction of the air outlet.
Push the air outlet adjustment knob outward to the maximum position to close the air outlet.
CAUTION
Do not place objects over the air vents.
Ensure the air intakes at the base of the windshield are clear of ice, snow or any other debris such as leaves. Blockage of the intakes may prevent air flow into the vehicle affecting the performance of the climate controls.
Air Temperature, Speed & Distribution
Temperature
The temperature of the air conditioning in the car can be adjusted through the infotainment screen.
To adjust the cabin temperature:
Use temperature control button on the climate bar to adjust temperature:
Tap  >  button to increase the temperature.
Tap  <  button to decrease the temperature.
NOTE
In cold conditions, it will take a few minutes for the heater to warm up and reach desired temperature.
Fan Speed
To adjust fan speed:
Tap  Fan Speed    button on the climate bar to open climate control interface.
Tap or slide the  slider  to change fan speed.
If the fan is switched  OFF , the climate control is also  Deactivated .
NOTE
After the air conditioning wind speed increases, the air conditioning noise will also increase.
Air Distribution
To adjust air distribution:
Tap  Fan Speed    button on the climate bar to open climate control interface.
Tap  blowing mode  button   to change air distribution.
Blowing mode:
The air conditioning airflow blows towards the front windshield and front side window
The air conditioning airflow blows towards the face
The air conditioning airflow blows towards the feet
NOTE
The face airflow and front windshield airflow cannot be used simultaneously.
Auto Operation
The Climate Control System controls the air speed, distribution, temperature and recirculation. In addition, it also governs the operating temperature cycle of the air conditioning﻿ system. When AUTO mode is activated, the system operates automatically.
To activate Auto mode:
Tap  Fan Speed    button on the climate bar to open climate control interface.
Tap  Auto  button to activate auto mode.
NOTE
The climate control system will switch from AUTO Mode to Manual Mode, if the fan speed, blower direction, A/C, Max A/C, or demist mode are changed manually.
In hot ambient conditions, recirculation may be automatically selected to increase the speed of cooling of the cabin and to reduce energy consumption.
For convenience and comfort the following is recommended:
Activate Auto mode.
For optimized air distribution, open all air vents.
Adjust the temperature as needed for the best comfort.
Manual Operation
The driver can control and adjust all climate control settings manually through the Infotainment screen.
To activate manual mode when auto mode is activated:
Manual mode is activated when any of the following settings are manually changed: fan speed, blower direction, A/C, Max A/C, or demist mode.
Operate the vehicle’s air conditioning through the Infotainment screen
Function
State On / Off
Action
Air Flow Distribution
Tap to switch air flow distribution mode
A/C
Tap to turn on/off front A/C
MAX A/C
Tap to turn on/off MAX A/C
Recirculation Mode
Tap to switch air inside/outside recirculation
Windshield Demist
Tap to activate/deactivate windshield demist
Temperature
Tap to decrease/increase the temperature
Fan Speed Adjustment
Tap or slide to adjust fan speed
SYNC
Tap to sync driver and passenger settings
Air Ionizer
Turn Air Ionizer mode ON and OFF
Front Windscreen Defrost and Defog
The front windscreen and a portion of the front windows can be defrosted or defogged using this function.
To turn on/off the front windscreen defrost/defog:
Tap  Front Defrost/Defog    button on climate bar to turn on/off front windscreen demist.
WARNING
A fogged windshield can reduce visibility and may result in serious injury or an accident for the driver.
NOTE
In warm and very humid ambient air conditions, the windscreen may mist up from the outside when cold air is directed at it. If the windscreen mists up from outside, activate windscreen wiper and demist.
Cabin Filtration
The Climate Control System filters the incoming air to remove dust, pollen, and contaminants. Replace the Cabin Air Filter as per the specified maintenance schedule, for better performance. See >   Fluids, Lubricants and Parts > Cabin Air Filter   for replacement instructions.
While driving through very dusty or polluted air, it is recommended to use the recirculation mode to reduce the amount of contaminants entering the vehicle cabin. See >   Climate Control System > Recirculation Mode  .
Air Ionizer
The Air Ionizer is a feature that adds ions to the air to trap air contaminants. Harmful particles and germs in the air can be reduced by ionization. This helps to improve air quality for individuals in the passenger compartment and aids in Cabin Filtration.
To activate the Air Ionizer:
Tap  Air Ionizer    button on the climate control interface to turn this feature On/Off.
NOTE
Regular cleaning of the vehicle interior is recommended, as this will help maintain the Cabin Air Filter and Climate Control System.
Recirculation Mode
Recirculation mode helps facilitate air circulation between the inside and outside of the vehicle cabin.
To adjust air distribution:
Tap  Fan Speed    button   on the climate bar to open climate control interface.
Tap  Recirculation mode  button   to change the circulation mode.
Recirculation mode:
When inside circulation mode is enabled, the existing cabin air inside the vehicle will be circulated and filtered.
When outside recirculation mode is enabled, fresh air will be drawn into the vehicle cabin from outside.
When Auto recirculation mode is enabled, recirculation mode is controlled automatically.
WARNING
If the inside circulation mode is on and cooling is off, this can cause air humidity to increase in the vehicle cabin, causing the windows to fog up which can be dangerous while driving.
CAUTION
You should not use the outside air intake mode when it’s raining, as there is a risk of bringing moisture or water into the car.
Do not continuously use Recirculation mode in Normal Conditions, as less fresh air is not drawn into the cabin and the quality of air inside the cabin can deteriorate with time.
NOTE
Keep the exterior air intake vents clean at all times, to ensure optimal performance of the climate control system.
Climate Control System Maintenance
Air Intake
The exterior air intake vents located in front of the windshield must be kept clean to allow air flow into the Climate Control System. Remove leaves, debris, dirt, and snow.
NOTE
It is not recommended to park the vehicle under trees with small leaves, as the leaves can easily clog the vehicle’s drainage system. If the vehicle must be parked under such trees, or if it is operated during periods of prolonged heavy rain, the drainage system should be inspected regularly and cleaned as necessary.
Do not park the vehicle beneath exposed roof drainage outlets, as the large volume of water may exceed the drainage capacity of the vehicle’s drain hoses.
Passenger compartment air filter maintenance
The air filter can effectively filter out impurities in the air, ensuring the normal operation of the air conditioning system. The air filter has a long service life and should be replaced regularly. For instructions on how to replace the cabin air filter, See >   Fluids, Lubricants and Parts > Cabin Air Filter  .
NOTE
If the vehicle is driven in highly polluted or dusty conditions, it is recommended to inspect and replace the filter more often.
Regular operation of the AC System
The air conditioning (AC) needs to be used for a few minutes each month, regardless of the weather and time of the year. This is to ensure that components within the Climate Control System remain lubricated.
Servicing of the Climate Control System
In order to obtain the best performance from the Climate Control System, it must be maintained properly and periodically. Refer to the Vehicle Maintenance Schedule for details, see >   Maintenance Schedule > Maintenance Schedule Overview  .
The maintenance includes the following:
Heating and cooling performance check.
Inspection for any leakage.
Replacement of the refrigerant and pressure testing, if required.
Cleaning of the condenser and evaporator drain tube.
Air filter replacement.
WARNING
Servicing must be completed by a trained and certified technician. The refrigerant is toxic and must be contained. Failure to follow the above may lead to serious injury or death.
CAUTION
Before any connection is disconnected, the refrigerant must be removed and recovered from the system by a trained and certified technician.
Use only the approved refrigerant and air-conditioning compressor oil. Using non approved material may lead to permanent damage to the vehicle.
Venting refrigerants directly to the atmosphere is harmful to the environment and may also create unsafe conditions based on inhalation, combustion, frostbite, or other health-based concerns.
Rear Demist (if equipped)
Rear windshield heating can be used for demisting.
To turn on/off rear windscreen demist:
Tap rear defrost   button on climate bar to turn on/off rear windscreen demist.
NOTE
The rear demister will automatically turn off.

Virtual Assistant Settings
To access Voice Assistant Settings﻿:

Or, Slide down from top of touchscreen to open Notification Center.

Voice Assistant Basic Mode Overview (VN Only):
Virtual Assistant’s Basic Mode offers a simple and standard way to interact with your vehicle. You can use voice commands to control basic vehicle functions and search for information. In addition, you can adjust basic settings to customize how Virtual Assistant interacts with you.
Default Voice  - Sets the default accent and gender for voice assistant responses.
Response Mode  - Controls interaction style.
Show Captions  - When Virtual Assistant responds to your commands, captions will always appear on the screen.
Show Your Speech  - When the vehicle detects your speech, it will display a transcription of your words on the screen for easy reference and confirmation.
Wake Phrase: “VinFast ơi” or “Hey VinFast”  - Say the wake phrase to activate Virtual Assistant and use voice commands.
Voice Welcome on Startup  - When the vehicle starts, Virtual Assistant greets you and proactively offers helpful guidance.
Follow-up Mode  - Enables the feature to ask follow-up questions without repeating the wake phrase.
Continued conversation time (seconds)  - Sets how long the assistant listens for follow-up questions after a response.
NOTE
Personalized Voice replaces ViVi’s default voice with your personal voice from the VinFast account linked to the vehicle’s login profile. This requires that you have created a personal voice in the VinFast app and set it as ViVi’s voice. An internet connection is needed to apply it.
Voice Assistant Advanced Mode Overview (VN Only):
Virtual Assistant’s Advanced Mode enhances your driving experience with a more personalized and intelligent voice assistant. Use your voice to effortlessly control specialized vehicle features. As a trusted companion, Virtual Assistant adapts to your preferences, recognizes your voice, provides proactive suggestions to make your drive smoother and more enjoyable.
Personalized Voice  -Replaces Virtual Assistant’s default voice with your personal voice from the VinFast account linked to the vehicle’s login profile.
Proactive Suggestion  – Enables Virtual Assistant to provide follow-up suggestions based on your previous command, making each interaction smoother and more efficient.
Favorite Topics  – Allows Virtual Assistant to offer more relevant suggestions and information tailored to you based on your selected preferred topics.
NOTE
A personal voice must be created in the VinFast app and set as Virtual Assistant’s voice. An internet connection is required to apply the voice.
Upgrade your VF Connect plan to access Advanced Virtual Assistant.

When starting vehicle, if the audio volume is high, the system will automatically decrease the volume to the Start-up Limit value. It is done to avoid scaring the driver.
The minimum volume of In-call and Start-up limit is 6.
The volume of the current playing source will be temporarily muted when an incoming phone call is received.

Geo-fence (if available)
Geo-fence creates a virtual geographic boundary around an area by means of GPS technology, the owner will receive a notification on the infotainment screen when the vehicle moves out of the geo-fence area.
Geo-fence settings can be viewed by all profiles except Valet. However, only the Primary profile can edit the Geo-fence settings.
This feature is helpful if somebody else is allowed to drive the vehicle and the owner wants to remind them that an agreed driving distance has been exceeded.

Upon exiting a Geo-fence, a notification will appear on the screen.

Time-fence is a time-period can be set beyond which if a vehicle is driven, a notification is sent by the Infotainment system.
Time-fence settings can be viewed by all profiles except Valet. However, only the Primary profile can edit the time-fence settings.
This feature is helpful if somebody else is allowed to drive the vehicle and the owner wishes to remind them that an agreed time-period has been exceeded.
Set the Time-fence limit using the VinFast app.
Moving beyond a Time-fence:
Upon moving beyond a Time-fence, a notification will appear on the Infotainment screen.

Valet Mode is a preset driver profile that limits speed and restricts access to advanced features. For detailed information, see >   Infotainment Screen > Valet Mode  .
Theft Alert
Theft Alert is a vehicle security feature that helps protect the vehicle from unauthorized access or movement while it is shut down.
Anti-theft Alarm  – Provides an alert through light and horn signals if a potential theft is detected. This feature is automatically activated when the vehicle is shut down (Light / Horn / Both).
GPS Monitoring  – Periodically checks the vehicle’s location during shutdown and triggers a theft alert if the vehicle is moved. This feature will be deactivated on the next start.

Warning levels of Child Presence Detection
Level 1:
A warning will be triggered and displayed on the infotainment screen if a child is detected sitting in any seat of the vehicle, all doors are closed, and no adults are present in the vehicle. The warning indicates that there is a child in the vehicle and information about the current temperature in the cabin.
Level 2:
The system will continue to sound warning and send a notification via VinFast App to alert the driver. The HVAC system will automatically turn ON to make the child comfortable.
Level 3:
The system will trigger an eCall warning if the battery state of charge becomes critically low (<15%) and the climate control system is no longer able to maintain a safe cabin temperature

# Battery & Charging


## High Voltage Components

High Voltage Components Overview
Power Distribution Unit, On Board Charging, DC-DC Converter
Charge Port
HV Battery
HV Heater (PTC)
HV Wiring
Electric Drive System (EDS)
HV Compressor
WARNING
Avoid contact with any high voltage components even when the battery is low or discharged as this can lead to fire, electric shock which can cause serious injury or death.
In case of an unlikely event of vehicle fire, contact local emergency responders, immediately.
Do not try to remove or replace any high voltage components, cables or connectors as the high voltage system has no user serviceable parts.
NOTE
For ease of identification, high voltage cables and wiring will have an Orange/Red colour covering together with information labels.
Read and follow all instructions on the labels that are attached to the vehicle. These are provided for your safety.

## Charging Instructions

Charging Equipment
The vehicle can be charged via a number of different charging devices. These include Portable Charger, Charging Stations, Super-Fast DC Charging Stations, and Home Chargers. The charging devices must have the correct charging adapter to plug into the vehicle.
Charge Plug
The vehicle is equipped with CCS2 (Common Charging System) connection. The available CCS2 connections are:
Portable charger (If equipped)
Portable charger provide slow charging for vehicles by converting AC electricity into DC electricity.
The portable charger controller comes with a charging indicator light. Please refer to the label on the back of the portable charger or the user guide provided with the portable charger for reference.
To use portable charger:
Park the vehicle correctly in a location that is convenient for charging.
Plug the portable charger into the power supply socket.
Open the charging port cover.
Remove the sealing cover of the charging port   and insert the portable charger   correctly into the charging port.
The vehicle will start charging, and charging information will be displayed on the infotainment screen.
If you need to stop charging midway, you can unlock the portable charger by clicking the“ Stop charging ” icon on the infotainment screen. Pull the portable charger out of the charging port, install the sealing cover of the charging port, and close the charging port cover.
NOTE
If the charging gun cannot be unlocked after clicking the button “Stop charging”, you can unlock the portable charger through emergency unlocking. See >   Charging Instructions > Charging Instructions  .
Remove the portable charger from the power socket and store it properly.
WARNING
Do not use power sockets that are significantly worn or damaged.
When charging, do not unplug the charging cable from the power socket.
Please charge in a relatively safe environment, avoid rain, water immersion, and keep away from sources of fire.
Do not allow children to engage in charging work.
After charging, do not disconnect the charging connection device when your hands are wet or standing in water on the ground.
NOTE
The portable charger will not work without a ground connection. The wall socket requires a ground pin to connect to the portable charger. This is required to safely ground the charging system and avoid short circuits.
For all indication light descriptions, refer to the label at the back of the portable charger or the user guide provided with portable charger.
Charging Stations
The vehicle can be charged at public charging stations. Charging stations may provide either AC or DC charging at various charging rates. Due to the variability between charging stations, it is critical to familiarize yourself with the operating instructions of each charging station correctly to prevent possible damage to the vehicle and to ensure safety.
Charge Station Identification
To ensure you charge your vehicle with compatible equipment, the charger-plug label displays a charge-type identifier (AC/DC) as illustrated below:
Charge Connector Type
Charge Type Identifier
A/C Charging
(Type 2)
DC Charging
(CCS2)
OR
WARNING
Failure to use a suitable charge station may lead to damage to the vehicles high voltage battery or charging system.
NOTE
The use of Super-Fast Charging Stations should be minimized to ensure the optimal lifetime performance of the battery.
Home Charger (If equipped)
A home charger can be installed at home or office to provide convenient charging. Typically, these will charge the vehicle quicker than the portable charger.
Home chargers need to be hard wired to the power supply in the home. Please contact a licensed electrician for installation.
Please contact a VinFast dealership for information on suitable VinFast - wall mounted home chargers.
Important Safety Warnings regarding Charging
WARNING
Contact with a high voltage power source can lead to serious injury or death.
Do not touch the contacts on the charging equipment or in the charging port of the vehicle.
Never use damaged, faulty or modified charging equipment.
Never use an extension cord, adapter or power board with charging equipment.
Protect the charging socket and contacts from water or moisture and other liquids.
The charger can generate electromagnetic waves that can affect medical devices such as an implantable cardiac pacemaker.
Always confirm with the medical team and manufacturer whether charging the electric vehicle will impact the operation of the medical devices such as a cardiac pacemaker.
There should be no water or dust in the charging handle connector and plug. Always make sure they are clean before connecting to the charger and charging inlet. Failure to do so may cause electric shock when connecting the charging handle or even lead to a fire.
Be careful not to touch or insert anything in the charging outlet holes, charging port holes, and the charging handle holes when connecting the charging handle to the charging port in the vehicle.
Immediately stop charging if abnormal symptoms are detected such as odor or smoke.
If the charge cable outer conduit is damaged then immediately replace the charging cable to prevent electrical shock.
Charging Instructions
Electric vehicles operate using electrical energy stored in a high-voltage battery. The high-voltage battery can be charged using any of the following methods:
Level I:  Charging from a grounded household power outlet using a portable charger. The actual charging efficiency may vary from the specifications listed on the charger label.
Level II:  Charging at AC charging stations, which allow users to charge faster compared to Level I charging.
Level III:  Charging at stations that use direct current (DC) power. DC fast chargers deliver higher current, enabling significantly faster charging compared to household outlets or Level II chargers.
WARNING
Chargers generate electromagnetic waves that can seriously affect medical electrical devices, such as implantable cardiac pacemakers. Consult your medical professional or pacemaker manufacturer to ensure that the charging of your electric vehicle will not impact the operation of your medical electrical devices.
Do not forcefully pull the charging handle from the charge port. It could result in dangerous electrical arcs, causing serious injury or death. Always follow the charging shut down process of the charging device and vehicle.
NOTE
Time required to charge the vehicle depends on the ambient and battery temperature, the power level of the charger, the charge level of the battery, and the charger type.
The speed of DC Charging Stations may be reduced in some circumstances, particularly when the ambient temperature is high. Hence charging can take more time.
To charge the vehicle follow the process below and the instructions of the charging device:
Stop the vehicle completely, put the vehicle into Park (P).
Check if the charging device is compatible with the vehicle.
Plug the portable charger into the power supply socket. (With portable charger).
Open the charge port door.
Fully insert the charging handle into the charge port, ensuring it is connected correctly.
Start the charging process at the charging device.
The charging handle will be automatically locked in the charge port during charging.
Observe the charging status on the vehicle Infotainment screen, and the charging device.
To stop charging, unlock the vehicle (if locked) and follow the instructions on the charging device and on the vehicle Infotainment screen.
The locking pin in the charge port will automatically disengage once charging has stopped. This may take some time after charging has been requested to stop.
Remove the charging handle from the charge port and return it to the charging device.
Close the charge port door.
NOTE When the vehicle is fully charged to 100% or reaches the target charger setting, the system will automatically stop charging.
Charge Port
The charging port door is located on the front right side of the vehicle. Open the charging port door to access the charging port.
To Open the Charge Port Door:
To open the charge port door, put the vehicle in Park (P), and unlock the charge port door by.
Press the charging port cover in the above shown location to open it.
WARNING
Do not touch the port while charging the vehicle.
NOTE
Due to freezing weather, it might be difficult to open the charge port door. Tap lightly on the door or remove any ice prior to attempting to open. Do not open the charge port door forcefully.
To plug in a charging handle
To charge the vehicle, firmly hold the charging handle (also known as the charging gun) and push it straight into the vehicle’s charging port. Make sure the connector is fully inserted and aligned evenly with the port. Improper connection may cause errors.
If you are using a mobile charger, connect it to the power source first before plugging it into the vehicle.
After plugging in the charging handle:
The system will start to authenticate the charging station. The same will also display on the Infotainment screen.
After a successful authentication, the vehicle will start charging.
WARNING
Ensure that there is no water, dust, dirt or debris in the charging handle connector and plug. Connecting while there is water, dust, dirt or debris in the charging handle and plug may cause a fire or an electric shock.
Be careful not to touch or insert anything in the charging outlet holes, charge port holes, and the charging handle holes when connecting to the Charge Port in the vehicle.
Immediately stop charging, if abnormal symptoms are detected such as an odor or smoke.
If the cable coating is damaged then immediately replace the charging cable to prevent electrical shock.
CAUTION
Make sure the charging connector and charging plug are always clean and dry. Keep the charging cable away from moisture or water.
NOTE If the battery temperature is high, there may be a slight delay before a charging session begins to allow the battery to cool down.
The charging will not start if the charging handle is not fully inserted and the locking pin is not engaged.
Refer to the Charging Faults section below for more information.
Charge port light status
The current charging status of the vehicle can be determined through the charging indicator light  .
The charging indicator light has the following states:
Charging in progress: Green light flashing.
Charging fault: Red light is always on.
Charging completed: Green light always on.
Standby/Not Connected: The indicator light is off.
Vehicle Charging Status
The following types of charging status will be shown on the Infotainment screen after plugging in the charger:
Remaining time   : The estimated time remaining to charge to the chosen set limit.
Current Battery SOC
Current Expected range
Battery temperature
Charge cable status   .
Battery charging status view   .
Stop charging   : To stop charging.
To stop charging from the Infotainment screen:
Tap  Stop charging   .
Confirm by tapping  Stop  button.
Wait a few seconds, the charging handle can be detached from charge port.
Charging will be stopped.
WARNING
Hold the charging handle and not the cable when connecting or removing the charging handle. Pulling the cable itself (without using the handle) may cause the internal wires to break, crack or get disconnected. This may cause electrical shock or fire.
During the charging process, the vehicle will automatically lock the charging handle. Do not use excessive force to pull out a locked charging handle, as this may cause serious injury, death or damage to the vehicle.
NOTE
Air conditioning performance may reduce in some circumstances during charging, particularly during high ambient temperature and high charging current. This is normal to ensure the battery cooling is optimized to maintain battery longevity.
Manual Release of the Charging handle:
The charging handle is automatically locked in the charge port by a locking pin to prevent accidental or ﻿unauthorized disconnection. If the usual method for releasing the charging handle from the charge port (using the Stop charging button) does not work, carefully follow the steps detailed below:
Ensure that the vehicle has stopped charging by checking the charging screen on the Infotainment screen.
Disconnect the power source (for charging stations, you may use the emergency stop button if available).
Turn the vehicle OFF and remove the key from the vehicle.
Open the front compartment.
Locate the charge port’s release cable   by removing the cover plate.
Use a tool (for example a screwdriver, hook, pliers) to pull the charge port’s release cable to release the pin that locks the charging handle.
Pull the charging handle from the charge port.
WARNING
To ensure safety when disconnecting the charging. Do not immediately pull the emergency release cable while the charging station is still powered - this may cause electric shock or arcing/fire. In such cases, it is recommended to press the emergency stop button on the charging station first before attempting to release charging handle.
NOTE
Use an appropriate tool to pull the release cable.
To close the Charge Port Door:
Close the charge port door and press in the location shown above to lock it.
CAUTION
Ensure the charging handle and cable are put away correctly, so that they do not cause a tripping hazard, or get caught in or damaged by the vehicle.
Do not drop the charging connector (charging handle). The charging connector can be damaged.
Do not attempt to remove the charging handle or the portable charger from the wall while the vehicle is charging. Doing so may damage the outlet and the cable.
Charging Faults
Charging Authentication Fault
An authentication fault may occur after connecting the charging handle to the vehicle. An authentication fault may occur when:
The charge station is not compatible with the vehicle.
The charging handle or charge station is damaged or not ready to charge.
If an authentication fault occurs, a message will be displayed on the Infotainment screen. Disconnecting and reconnecting the charging handle or using an alternate charger may correct the authentication issue. Follow all messages shown on the Infotainment screen.
If charging does not commence once the charging handle is connected to the vehicle, please try switching to other charging station, if the issue persists, follow the below process:
Remove the charging handle from the charge port
Close the charge port door
Ensure the vehicle is OFF
Lock the vehicle
Wait for 5 minutes for the vehicle to enter Sleep mode
Unlock the vehicle
Open the charge port door
Reconnect the charging handle
Check Charging Status
If the issue persists, contact the nearest VinFast Service Center.
Charging handle Fails to Release
If you are unable to remove the charging handle from the charging port after charging, the locking latch may not have released from the handle. Manually release the latch to unlock the charging handle.

## Battery Information

Battery Information
The vehicle is equipped with a High Voltage (HV) battery located beneath the floor and a 12V Low Voltage (LV) battery positioned under the hood/in the front compartment. If the 12V battery or HV battery needs to be inspected, please contact the nearest VinFast Service Center.
Ensuring the battery systems are in good condition helps maintain optimal vehicle performance.
CAUTION
Please charge the vehicle immediately if the high voltage battery state of charge is less than 5% (A notification will be displayed on the screen). The battery warning threshold can be adjusted through the Infortamaint screen, see >   EV Battery App  .
Avoid discharging the battery to 0% as this may cause the battery health to degrade more quickly.
NOTE
The vehicle uses energy from the High voltage battery to recharge the 12V battery.
The actual driving range may vary depending on operating conditions, including but not limited to driving behavior, vehicle load, traffic conditions, road conditions, weather, and battery condition.
EV Battery App
The EV Battery App shows detailed information about the Vehicle’s High Voltage Battery and Charging System.
To access the EV Battery App:
Tap  App Library    >  EV App   .
OR, Tap  Battery  in the Driver View Area, if available.

Battery Care
The high voltage battery requires periodic inspection as per the VinFast checklist. Always pay attention to the battery status and never allow it to fully discharge. Even when the vehicle is not being driven, the battery discharges very slowly to power the on board electronics. The discharge rate may vary depending on environmental factors (such as cold weather), configuration, and selected settings.
If the battery is not used for an extended period (2–3 weeks), always maintain the state of charge above 30% (preferable between 30% to 60%).
If the high voltage battery needs to be inspected, tested, or replaced, it should only be done by a trained technician. If the 12-volt or high voltage battery needs to be serviced, contact your nearest VinFast Service Center.
Factors Contributing to High Battery Consumption:
Driving at higher speeds.
Extremes in temperature and strong winds.
Adjusting cabin temperature through heating or cooling.
Driving a steep incline.
Frequent starts and stops, typically in heavy traffic jam conditions.
Carrying heavy loads.
Driving with opened windows.
Improper care of wheels and tires.
Additional accessories or modifications that impact aerodynamics.
Operational factors that may affect battery lifespan
During vehicle operation, certain driving habits may subject the high-voltage battery to elevated temperatures and high electrical loads for extended periods, which may affect battery life and performance.
Frequent sudden acceleration
Repeated hard acceleration or aggressive throttle input can cause the battery to deliver high current over a short period, increasing battery temperature and stress on the battery cells.
Recommendation: Accelerate gradually and drive smoothly to help maintain stable battery operation and extend battery life.
Parking in High-Temperature Environments for Extended Periods
High ambient temperatures, especially when the vehicle is parked under direct sunlight for extended periods, can increase battery temperature and accelerate battery aging
Recommendation: Whenever possible, park the vehicle in a shaded, well-ventilated area to help protect the battery.
Operating the Vehicle with the Battery at High Temperature for Extended Periods
Continuous operation at elevated battery temperatures can accelerate side reactions inside the battery cells, reducing battery lifespan.
Recommendation: Reduce aggressive acceleration, drive gently, and allow sufficient time for the battery to cool down when necessary.
WARNING
Maintain a safe distance from the vehicle in case of damage to the high voltage battery or other high voltage components as there is a high risk of electric shock, overheating, or fire. Failure to comply may cause serious injuries or death.
If the vehicle is damaged from flood, crash, fire, or other event, it should be inspected by an ﻿authorized service technician as soon as possible. The vehicle should be kept outside until it has been inspected at a distance of at least 15m (50ft) from any structure or anything that can burn. Open a window or door of the vehicle to allow air to circulate inside.
CAUTION
It is recommended to charge the battery when the State of Charge (SOC) drops below 20%, and to charge it immediately when the SOC falls below 5%. Do not allow the high-voltage battery to become fully depleted (0%), as this may reduce battery life and performance.
Storing the vehicle for more than 6 months without carrying out necessary maintenance may permanently damage the HV battery.
Do not store or park the vehicle near heat source due to risk of catching fire, which can damage the battery and vehicle.
Do not store the vehicle in area which may flood as flood may damage the battery and the vehicle.
NOTE
The High Voltage battery SOC shall always be more than 30% for storage (preferable between 30% to 60%). If you plan to store the vehicle for 3~6 months, battery maintenance is recommended.
For optimal life and performance of the battery, minimize the use of super-fast DC charging.
For optimal battery life, avoid charging it to 100% SOC on a regular basis; set the charging limit to 80-90%, and only charge to full manually when necessary.
If the vehicle is damaged from flood, crash, fire, or any other event, it may be necessary to have the vehicle inspected, and the battery may need to be replaced. If the battery is involved in a fire, it may need to be replaced and returned to manufacturer for investigation.
If the vehicle is unresponsive and does not unlock, open, or charge, the 12V battery may be discharged. In this situation, try jump starting the 12V battery. If the vehicle is still unresponsive, contact VinFast Service Center.
Charge the battery fully (100%) once a month for optimal performance. For any concerns, please contact a VinFast Service Center.
Temperature Limits
Avoid exposing the vehicle to ambient temperatures above 55°C (131°F) or below -30°C (-22°F). Ideally, store the vehicle in a climate controlled garage if extreme ambient temperatures are expected. Failure to follow this temperature recommendation may permanently degrade battery performance.
Low Power Consumption Mode
To protect against complete High Voltage battery discharge, the vehicle enters a low power consumption mode when the charge level is very low. In this mode, the HV battery stops supporting the on-board electronics to slow the discharge rate. Once this low power consumption mode is active, it is important to charge the vehicle as soon as possible to avoid possible battery damage.
When the low-power consumption mode is active, the 12V battery is no longer charged and can completely discharge. If this occurs, the 12V battery will need to be charged or replaced before commencing any High Voltage charging of the vehicle. To conserve energy, select the ECO driving mode from Driver View Area.
To select ECO driving mode:
Adjusting ECO driving mode, see >   Drive Systems > Drive Modes  .
CAUTION
Do not allow the High Voltage battery to discharge completely. There is a possibility that the High Voltage battery will no longer charge if left discharged for long periods of time.

# Maintenance


## General Information

Vehicle Care Overview
To ensure a trouble-free experience with the vehicle, regular maintenance should be carried out as stipulated in the Owner’s Manual. If any abnormal sounds or vibrations are observed, visit the nearest VinFast Service ﻿Center to have the vehicle inspected. Regular maintenance and servicing are recommended to prevent operational problems with the vehicle.
CAUTION
All service procedures must be carried out by an authorized VinFast technician.

## Fluids, Lubricants and Parts

Cabin Air Filter
The air conditioner cabin filter prevents dust, pollen, and other pollutants from entering the vehicle. ﻿To achieve the best filtering effects, replace the cabin filter within the specified maintenance interval specified by VinFast.
The cabin air filter should be replaced according to the Maintenance Schedule. If the vehicle is operated in severely air polluted or dusty conditions, inspect and replace the cabin air filter more frequently.
NOTE
Filter life depends on atmospheric conditions.
Filter Replacement
To remove the Cabin Air filter:
The installation position of the air filter is located under the glove box.
Open the glove box and carefully push in the side clips   to disengage the glove box.
Disconnect the damper   on the right side of the glove box.
Remove the glove box mounting frame bottom cover   by pressing and releasing the retaining tab on the right side.
Remove the air filter cover   by pressing and releasing the retaining tabs on the right side.
Remove the air filter   by pulling it out.
To install the Cabin Air filter:
To install a new air filter, reassemble in the reverse order of removal process.
Coolant
The vehicles cooling system contains coolant fluid to maintain optimum operating temperature for certain components in the vehicle.
To ensure efficient cooling is maintained, it is recommended that the cooling system is periodically inspected for coolant leakage and/or damage.
To perform the inspection, the vehicle should be parked on level ground and the coolant and cooling system must be allowed to cool down.
Use only approved coolant fluid for electric vehicles. Adding other types of water or antifreeze may damage the vehicle.
Coolant replacement should be carried out by a qualified VinFast technician.
For recommended coolant, see >   Technical Specifications > Fluids, Lubricants and Parts Overview  .
To top-up the coolant:
Park the vehicle on level surface. Turn OFF the vehicle. Allow the front compartment components to completely cool off.
Open and secure the hood.
Remove the front trunk.
Locate the coolant reservoirs.
Slowly unfasten the filler cap   by turning it in an anti-clockwise direction, if a “hiss” sound is audible, stop turning the filler cap   and wait until the sound stops and then proceed to remove it.
Refill the coolant reservoir using Anti-Freeze Pre-Mixed Coolant AF2100 (ASTM D3306) 50% glycol and 50% water mixture.
The coolant level should be between the  MIN  and  MAX  indicator marks as shown on the side of the reservoir.
Tighten the filler cap   by turning it in a clockwise direction.
WARNING
Do not open the coolant reservoir cap when the vehicle is ON or when the cooling system is still hot. Let the cooling system completely cool before removing the cap. Remove the coolant reservoir cap slowly and cover it with a thick cloth to prevent scalding. Failure to following these instructions may result in a serious injury.
Coolant is extremely toxic and can be fatal if swallowed. Keep coolant bottles sealed and out of reach of children. If a child has accidentally ingested the coolant, seek immediate medical attention.
Handle the coolant carefully and avoid contact with skin and eyes. In case, the coolant comes in/into contact with skin or eyes, rinse with plenty of water immediately.
Be careful when changing coolant, as there are High Voltage components in this area.
Do not touch heater, radiator, A/C pipes or hoses. They may be very hot and may cause severe burns.
Do not run the vehicle if there is a leak in the coolant reservoir, all coolant could leak out causing a fire or leading to severe burns. Any leaks must be fixed by an ﻿authorized VinFast Service Center before driving the vehicle.
Keep hands, clothing, and tools away from the rotating fan of the radiator to avoid injury.
CAUTION
Keep coolant in a sealed container in a safe place.
Do not fill the coolant reservoir with excess coolant. Coolant left on a metallic surface for extended periods of time may cause rusting.
NOTE
Ensure the cap and reservoir are clean and free from debris before opening the coolant reservoir to prevent any foreign particles from entering the reservoir.
Open both coolant tank cap and top-up both tanks to maintain recommended coolant level
Washer Fluid
Washer Fluid is used for cleaning the windshield with the operation of the windshield wipers. The washer fluid reservoir is located under the hood of the vehicle. Check the washer fluid level regularly and add fluid if required.
To Top-up the Washer Fluid﻿:
Locate the washer fluid reservoir by removing the cover plate  .
Pull open the washer fluid cap.
Add washer fluid to reservoir until the reservoir is full.
Refit the cap to close the washer fluid reservoir, ensuring it is tightly fitted.
WARNING
Do not touch any high voltage components near washer fluid reservoir area.
Washer fluid can irritate eyes and skin. Read and follow the instructions provided by the washer fluid manufacturer.
In cold climates where water can freeze, use a washer fluid with anti-freeze agent. In colder regions, using a washer fluid without an anti-freeze agent can impair visibility through the windshield.
Washer fluid is toxic and can be injurious if swallowed. Keep washer fluid bottles sealed and out of reach of children. If a child has accidentally ingested the washer fluid, seek immediate medical attention.
Keep washer fluid away from children and animals.
CAUTION
Make sure to use windshield washer fluid specifically designed for automobiles. Using other substances, such as untreated water, may cause damage to the vehicle.
Add washer fluid carefully, do not let it splash on to the exterior surface of the vehicle.
Do not turn the wiper ON without using water or washer fluid as it may scratch or damage the windshield when dry.
The concentration of anti-freeze agent in the washer fluid must be adjusted to the vehicle operating conditions and the climate. A high concentration may lead to vehicle damage.
Do not mix water with ready-to-use washer fluid. Water can cause the solution to freeze and damage the washer fluid tank and other parts of the washer system.
Fill the washer fluid reservoir only three-quarters full when it is very cold. This allows for fluid expansion if freezing occurs, which could damage the reservoir if it is completely full.
NOTE
When using concentrated washer fluid, follow the manufacturer instructions for adding water.
Wiper Blades
Windshield Wiper Blades should be regularly inspected for wear or cracking. Contamination of either the windshield or the wiper blades with foreign matter can reduce the effectiveness of the windshield wipers.
Common sources of contamination are:
Insects
Tree sap
If the wiper blades are not wiping/cleaning the windshield properly, clean both the windshield and the wiper blades with a mild detergent, and rinse thoroughly with clean water.
Wiper blades will degrade over time and eventually require replacement. If water is not sufficiently cleared from the windshield and blades squeak when in operation, wiper blade replacement may be required.
Front Wiper Blade Replacement
The vehicle needs to be in ACC power mode.
Wipers need to be in home position, wiper stalk in OFF position.
Set Wiper Service Mode to ON, see >   Settings >Vehicle Settings  .
Lift the wiper arm away from the windshield.
Press and hold the locking to release the wiper blade as shown in the illustration, pull the wiper blade in the direction of the arrow to remove it from the wiper arm assembly.
Slide the new wiper blade on the wiper arm in the direction shown until it clicks into place on the wiper arm.
Place the wiper assembly back on the windshield and check that the wiper blade is properly attached to the wiper arm.
Turn Wiper Service mode OFF from the Infotainment screen to return the Wiper back to the home position.
Rear Wiper Blade Replacement
The vehicle needs to be in ACC power mode.
Wipers need to be in home position, wiper stalk in OFF position.
Set Wiper Service Mode to ON, see >   Settings >Vehicle Settings  .
Lift the wiper arm away from the rear window. Remove the old wiper blade.
Install the new wiper blade.
Place the wiper assembly back on the windshield and check that the wiper is properly attached to the wiper arm.
Turn Wiper Service mode OFF from the Infotainment screen to return the Wiper back to the home position.
CAUTION
Wiper blades cannot be locked into a lifted position. Do not lift a wiper arm beyond its intended position as it can cause damage to wiper arm.
Do not use or allow wiper arms to touch the windshield without installing wiper blades. This can damage the windshield. Such damage would not be covered by the vehicle warranty.
NOTE
Non genuine products should not be used. Using non genuine wiper blades may damage the wiper system and windshield.
Brake Pads & Fluids
Brake pads & brake fluid are important components of the vehicle’s braking system. To ensure effective braking performance it is important to regularly inspect the brake pads for excessive wear and check the brake fluid level.
Brake Pads
Brake pad wear is affected by various vehicle operating conditions and increased pad wear may occur if the vehicle is driven in an aggressive manner with frequent and heavy breaking.
The brake pad is equipped with brake pad wear indicators. The wear indicator is a thin metal strip attached to the brake pad that produces a squealing sound when it makes contact with the disc brake, indicating that the brake pad material has reached the end of its effective service life.
Please contact your nearest VinFast Service Center to have the vehicle’s braking system inspected if you are facing difficulties with stopping the vehicle effectively.
WARNING
Do not neglect to service the vehicle braking system as it may result in reduced brake system performance, increasing the stopping distance which may lead to an accident.
Do not drive with worn-out brake pads. Damage to the braking system can result in a braking hazard.
The braking effect may get reduced if the brake pads become wet after heavy rainfall or immediately after washing the vehicle. This might require pressing the brake pedal with more force to compensate.
In high-salinity environments, a salt layer on the brake pads may affect the braking performance of the vehicle.
Overheated brakes reduce the braking effect and considerably increase the braking distance.
NOTE
Always replace brake pads as a set, either both front or both rear sets.
To avoid costly brake repairs, do not continue to drive with worn-out brake pads.
Brake Fluid
Brake fluid is a type of hydraulic fluid used in hydraulic brake systems to transfer force into pressure.
The brake fluid level in the reservoir should be checked regularly. Brake fluid may absorb some amount of moisture from the atmosphere over time. The hydraulic braking system may get damaged if there is too much moisture in the braking fluid.
To Check the Brake Fluid:
Ensure the vehicle is parked on a flat surface.
Let the braking system cool down.
Open and secure the hood.
Locate the brake fluid reservoir by removing the cover plate  .
The fluid level should be between the MIN and MAX indicator marks as shown on the side of the reservoir.
There are two reasons why the brake fluid level in the reservoir may go down:
Brake pads normal wear. When new pads are installed, the fluid level goes back up.
Fluid leak in the hydraulic brake system. Have the hydraulic brake system fixed. With a leak, the brakes will not work well.
Always clean the brake fluid reservoir cap and the area around the cap before removing and installing the cap.
Check the brake fluid level regularly and keep it near the MAX level. Do not let the fluid level get close to the MIN level, as this may cause a low brake fluid warning to appear in some driving conditions, such as driving on steep roads or making sharp turns for a long time. If the brake fluid level drops unusually or the low brake fluid warning comes on, have the vehicle checked at a VinFast Service Center as soon as possible.
CAUTION
Do not mix other types of brake fluid, otherwise it will cause damage to the braking system and components.
NOTE
The brake fluid used in this vehicle is DOT 4.
The red brake indicator   on the information entertainment screen will illuminate if the brake fluid in the reservoir drops below the recommended level. The ABS indicator   may also illuminate. Please contact your nearest VinFast Service Center immediately to have the vehicle’s braking system inspected.
WARNING
The brake fluid level must be between MIN and MAX level. Brake fluid may leak out over the edge of the reservoir if the brake fluid level is above the MAX indicator and may result in damage to the vehicle.
If the brake light illuminates while driving, stop as soon as safely possible and check the brake fluid level. If left unchecked, this may prevent effective braking and cause increased movement in the brake pedal and/or complete brake failure.
Brake fluid is highly toxic. Keep brake fluid container sealed and out of reach of children. Seek immediate medical attention if accidentally exposed to any brake fluid.
Prevent brake fluid from making contact with skin and eyes. If contact is made, rinse immediately with water. If redness, pain, or discomfort persists, seek immediate medical attention.
Do not touch any of the High Voltage components in the brake fluid reservoir area.
Do not use ﻿unauthorized products. Usage of incorrect or contaminated brake fluid could result in damaging the braking system. This could result in the loss of braking, leading to an accident.
CAUTION
Use brake fluid only from a sealed airtight container. Do not use previously used fluid or fluid from an opened container, as brake fluid has a tendency to absorb moisture which decreases braking performance.
Do not allow any brake fluid to come into contact with the vehicle paint to avoid corrosion.
Contact VinFast Service Center﻿ immediately if any increased movement of the brake pedal or a significant loss of brake fluid is observed. Driving under these conditions can result in extended stopping distances or complete brake failure.
NOTE
The brake fluid level drops slightly during vehicle operation and as the brake pads wear.
Air Conditioner
To ensure continuous and efficient operation of the air conditioning system, a few minutes of cooling must be regularly performed once a month, regardless of the weather conditions and time of the year.
The air conditioning system in the vehicle is filled with R134a﻿ refrigerant. The technical details of the air conditioning refrigerant can be found on the label inside the hood of the vehicle.﻿ See >   Fluids, Lubricants and Parts > Fluids, Lubricants and Parts Overview  .
WARNING
Air conditioning systems contain high-pressured refrigerants. Do not open the air conditioner refrigerant system as it may result in serious injuries.
Air conditioning refrigerant systems should only be serviced by an authorized service technician.
NOTE
Always use manufacturer ﻿authorized refrigerant and air-conditioning compressor oil.
In humid climates, the air conditioning may create water droplets inside the vehicle. It is recommended to use the air conditioner with the windows closed.
Electric vehicles run much quieter than gasoline-powered vehicles, therefore the A/C compressor may be more audible.

## Electrical System

Fuses and Circuit Breakers
The vehicle is equipped with fuses and circuit breakers to prevent electrical system overload. The fuse box in the vehicle is located under the right front engine hood, at the top of the 12 volt battery, and behind the driver’s side dashboard. If the fuse/relay blows, it is strongly recommended that only VinFast technicians replace or inspect the electrical fuse.
When a fuse blows, the element inside the fuse is melted or broken. Always replace a fuse with fuse of identical size and rating. If the replaced fuse blows immediately after replacing, contact the local VinFast Service Center for maintenance as soon as possible.
WARNING
Fuses and circuit breakers are marked with their ampere rating. Do not exceed the specified rating when replacing fuses and circuit breakers. The use of an oversized fuse or circuit breaker can result in a vehicle fire causing serious injury or death.
Installing fuses that do not meet VinFast’s original fuse specifications is dangerous. The fuses could fail, resulting in a fire causing vehicle damage and/or death.
Spilling liquid on any electrical component in the vehicle may damage it. Always be very careful when working around electrical components.
CAUTION
A fuse must not be replaced with anything but another fuse of same size and rating.
Never try to repair a blown fuse.
Do not use copper wire or aluminum foil as a temporary substitute for a proper fuse. Such substitutes can cause extensive wiring damage, possibly resulting in a fire.
Avoid using a screwdriver or any other metal objects to remove fuses, as they may cause short circuits.
IP Fusebox
Instrument Panel fuse box is located in the lower left-hand side of the dashboard.
To access Front IP fusebox:
Turn the vehicle OFF.
Make sure all switches are in the OFF position.
Use a suitable tool to disengage the 7 clips, then remove the instrument panel left cover.
Use a suitable tool to remove the 2 screws.
Use a suitable tool to disengage the 8 clips, then remove the left dashboard guard plate.
Refer to the instructions detail below.
Remove the blown fuse out by using the removal tool which is provided in the front compartment fuse box.
Check and replace the blown fuse.
Make sure the replaced fuse has the same rating and it fits tightly in its place. If in doubt, please contact a VinFast service technician.
